docs\4class-memory-taxonomy-discipline.md:71:## Boundary precedence (Pattern A fix-forward edit #2 per codex T1 prescription)
docs\4class-memory-taxonomy-discipline.md:109:- **Promote OPERATIONAL-RUNBOOK directly to PROCEDURAL without intermediate evidence-synthesis** — refuted by cycle-322 + evidence-synthesis discipline below. See §Evidence-synthesis ladder (Pattern A fix-forward edit #3 + #5): promotion does NOT mutate the source record; it creates a NEW higher-order record (Class 1 or Class 3) with backlinks to supporting Class 4 / Class 2 records. The ladder is therefore an EVIDENCE-SYNTHESIS ladder, NOT a reclassification ladder.
docs\4class-memory-taxonomy-discipline.md:110:- **Reclassify a memory record by changing its class label** (Pattern A fix-forward edit #5 per codex T1 prescription) — refuted by storage-class-immutability. A SEMANTIC-FACT record once written stays Class 1 even after additional evidence accumulates; what changes is that a SEPARATE new record at a different class is created. Storage class does NOT mutate historical records.
docs\4class-memory-taxonomy-discipline.md:111:- **Auto-promote EPISODIC-TRACE to PROCEDURAL** (extended per Pattern A fix-forward edit #4) — refuted by Fire 29c "promote knowledge only when evidence SURVIVES REPLAY" + cycle-322 evidence-synthesis. **EPISODIC-TRACE NEVER auto-promotes directly to PROCEDURAL.** Multiple episodic traces (n≥3 same-arc) MAY SERVE AS Gate 4 / cycle-322 EVIDENCE for a SEPARATE procedural codification ship, but the procedural codification is a NEW Class 3 record, NOT a reclassification of the Class 2 records.

.claude\rules\citation-discipline.md:42:6. **TIER-1-NAMED-AUTHOR-QUOTE class for inherited patterns** — when a SOTA pattern is derived from a named author's published work (book / peer-reviewed paper / conference talk), cite verbatim-quote + author-name + publication inline. The named-author cite is TIER-1 primary-source for the PATTERN (not the implementing repo); use alongside repo file:line cites for full provenance. Exemplar: `Z:/repos/deps/mattpocock/skills/README.md` (48,857★, MIT, [VERIFIED 2026-04-30 PM via curl raw GitHub]) quotes Pragmatic Programmer (Thomas & Hunt) for "rate of feedback is your speed limit" (failure-mode #3 framing), Domain-Driven-Design (Eric Evans) for ubiquitous-language pattern (#2 framing), and Extreme Programming Explained (Kent Beck) for "invest in design every day" (#4 framing). Per `.claude/projects/Z--claude-sota-installed/memory/reference_mattpocock_skills_pattern_extract_2026_04_30.md` for the full mining trace + adoption-candidate ranking. **TIER-1-NAMED-AUTHOR-QUOTE PROVISIONAL-EXCEPTION** *(round-3 framing per T3 round-2 conf=0.88 ESCALATING finding on commit `d4df352`; supersedes round-1 "full cardinal-rule-1 satisfaction" framing — physical-only sources do NOT fully satisfy committed-tree inspectability)*: physical-only printed-book/journal/conference-publication primary anchor (ISBN + page + verbatim-quote) is a PROVISIONAL-EXCEPTION to cardinal-rule-1 (CLAUDE.md L7), NOT full satisfaction. Cite class downgraded to TIER-1-NAMED-AUTHOR-QUOTE-PROVISIONAL with REQUIRED metadata: (a) ISBN/DOI + page + verbatim-quote inline; (b) verifier-identity disclosure (who-verified-when, e.g., `[VERIFIED 2026-05-04 by orchestrator against book Tip #15 verbatim text]`); (c) explicit `non-automatable: true` marker so review hooks know to defer to human sign-off; (d) **HARD-GATE ELIGIBILITY TEST** *(Round-3 amendment a per T3 R3 NEEDS-ATTENTION conf=0.82 same-class finding on commit `1b87a6e`; supersedes round-2 "MUST prefer" soft preference framing — preference is not gateable; eligibility is)*: PROVISIONAL admission is admissible ONLY when DOI landing page + accessible PDF / publisher excerpt / archived scan (license-permitting) was SEARCHED AND UNAVAILABLE. The commit body MUST document the search (which DOI/publisher/archive was queried + return shape — e.g., "searched ACM DOI 10.1145/<doi> 2026-05-05 — paywall-only, no excerpt found; searched Internet Archive — no scan; searched publisher excerpt page — none"). Mere preference is INSUFFICIENT — if any auditable surrogate exists (DOI/PDF/excerpt/scan), use it as the normative source instead of the physical-only book cite. e.g., ACM DOI `https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/<doi>` for ACM papers IS auditable when paywall-free. Secondary aggregator URLs (Wikipedia, `https://pragprog.com/tips/`, `https://martinfowler.com/bliki/<topic>.html`, `c2.com/cgi/wiki`) are VERIFICATION PROXIES only — they help re-verify the primary quote without owning the book physically, but are NOT cited as normative source. **Independent named-reviewer sign-off mandate** *(Round-3 amendment b per T3 R3 NEEDS-ATTENTION conf=0.82 same-class finding on commit `1b87a6e`; supersedes round-2 "Lower-confidence reviewer-sign-off mandate" sole-orchestrator framing — sole-orchestrator verification is no longer admissible)*: rule edits citing TIER-1-NAMED-AUTHOR-QUOTE-PROVISIONAL MUST cite a SECOND verifier-identity BEYOND the orchestrator who replayed the verbatim quote against the source — commit body MUST embed BOTH verifier-identity-1 (the orchestrator, e.g., `[VERIFIED 2026-05-05 by orchestrator against book Tip #15 verbatim text]`) AND verifier-identity-2 (the independent named reviewer, e.g., `[VERIFIED 2026-05-05 by <human-name> against same book + same page + same quote]`) with explicit `[VERIFIED yyyy-mm-dd by <name> against <source>]` markers for each. The 2-verifier requirement closes the "self-attestation only" gap that round-2 left open. **`non-automatable: true` operator-discipline mandate (manual-only; no hook enforcement YET)** *(Round-4 amendment per T3 round-4 NEEDS-ATTENTION conf=0.82 SAME-CLASS finding on commit `d07ac71`; supersedes Round-3 amendment c "BLOCKING in T3/T4/T6 verdict-shape" claim that was prose-only without hook implementation — codex GPT-5.5 BRIDGE-MODE caught the policy/enforcement drift: rule text claimed automated BLOCKING but no T3/T4/T6 hook, verdict parser, schema, or test changed. Per closed-loop-recursive-narrowing.md §Outcome A monotone-decline path, downgraded prose closes the drift while the enforcement-hook ship is queued separately)*: when a TIER-1-NAMED-AUTHOR-QUOTE-PROVISIONAL cite is shipped, the operator MUST: (i) embed `non-automatable: true` marker inline; (ii) embed explicit "no automated enforcement, human gate only" disclosure in commit body acknowledging that automated re-verification is impossible AND naming both verifier-identities (per amendment b); (iii) accept residual provenance gap explicitly. The discipline is OPERATOR-MANUAL — NO T3/T4/T6 hook currently parses `non-automatable: true` as BLOCKING. Promotion to mechanical BLOCKING is FORWARD-REF for a separate enforcement-hook ship: `tools/non_automatable_blocking_audit.py` parsing committed rule edits + emitting BLOCKING status in T3/T4/T6 verdict-shape + regression tests at `tests/test_non_automatable_blocking_audit.py`. Do NOT claim BLOCKING semantic in commit bodies until that ship lands. Forward-only convention codified Wave 18 PSW arc — Bundle B Round-2 commit `d6acfb1`, Bundle B Round-3 commit `217430f`, karpathy 5-author commit `0fe6282`, citation-discipline Round-2 DEMOTE commit `1b87a6e`, citation-discipline Round-3 hard-gate commit `d07ac71`, this Round-4 prose-downgrade commit closes the architectural concern per `reference_wave18_psw_arc_close_4ship_2026_05_04.md` + Outcome A monotone-decline path per `closed-loop-recursive-narrowing.md` (R1 conf=0.86 → R2 conf=0.88 ESCALATING → R3 conf=0.82 DECLINING → R4 conf=0.82 SAME-CLASS DECLINING-STABLE; severity stable medium; severity-gate satisfied via concrete-verification mechanism per amendments a+b + prose-downgrade per amendment c; arc converging within Round 5 ceiling).

.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:17:**Distinct source definition** (per Pattern A codex T1 Fire 29a prescriptions #1+#2+#6): A "distinct source" is a distinct **backend / data corpus / provider**, NOT a tool endpoint. Multiple MCP endpoints OR CLI commands under the SAME provider count as ONE source family. Example: `mcp__github__search_repositories` + `mcp__github__search_code` + `mcp__github__list_commits` + `gh CLI` all count as ONE source (GitHub).
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:75:- **Fix-forward / Pattern A apply** on existing codex T1 verdict — no new discovery needed; the verdict's prescriptions are the input
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:88:- **Counting multiple endpoints from one provider as multiple distinct sources** (per Pattern A codex T1 Fire 29a prescription #6) — refuted by §Discovery cohort "Distinct source definition" + §Counting rules: a single provider (GitHub / DeepWiki / Exa / Context7 / repomix) collapses ALL its sub-capabilities into ONE source-family count. Example anti-pattern: querying `mcp__github__search_repositories` + `mcp__github__search_code` + `mcp__github__list_commits` + `gh CLI repo view` and claiming "4 sources" — actual count = 1 source (GitHub family). Must query ≥4 DIFFERENT provider families.
.claude\rules\multi-source-discovery-breadth-discipline.md:89:- **Counting INSTALLED-NOT-WIRED sources as available without verifying alternate path** (per Pattern A codex T1 Fire 29a prescription #4+#5) — refuted by §Counting rules: NOT-WIRED sources require documented working alternate path (WebFetch / HTTP-direct / ctx_fetch_and_index) AND relevant-results return. Example anti-pattern: Perplexity@0.8.4 npm-global is INSTALLED but NOT in current .mcp.json; cannot count as "MCP source" until either wired OR queried via WebFetch fallback with relevant results.

docs\cosign-verify-attestation-discipline.md:252:- **Accept a valid Sigstore signature WITHOUT checking artifact digest + cert identity against expected upstream** (Pattern A fix-forward edit #7 per codex T1 #7 prescription) — refuted: Sigstore signatures only attest "this artifact was signed by this identity at this time"; they do NOT validate the artifact is FROM your expected upstream/repo/workflow. Verification logic MUST cross-check: (a) artifact SHA-256 digest matches expected hash, (b) certificate subject matches `^https://github\.com/<expected-owner>/<expected-repo>/` regex, (c) workflow path matches `.github/workflows/<expected-workflow>.yml@` when known. Missing any cross-check = effectively unsigned.
docs\cosign-verify-attestation-discipline.md:253:- **Use broad `--certificate-identity-regexp` matching forks / unrelated workflows / user-controlled refs when tighter identity is knowable** (Pattern A fix-forward edit #8 per codex T1 #8 prescription) — refuted: open regexp like `^https://github\.com/<owner>/<repo>/` matches ALL workflows in repo including PR-checks workflows, dependabot workflows, fork-rebased workflows (if attacker submits a PR with malicious release workflow). When the expected workflow path IS known (e.g., `.github/workflows/release.yml`), MANDATE tightening: `^https://github\.com/<owner>/<repo>/\.github/workflows/release\.yml@refs/tags/v\d+\.\d+\.\d+$`. Forbid org-only OR unanchored regexps unless explicit provenance documentation justifies (e.g., multi-workflow release strategy intentionally varies workflow path).
